YORUNGE SIM: RUST THIRD-PARTY LICENSES AND NOTICES
================================================

Scope: the locked normal/build dependency graph selected for this Windows build,
including procedural macros and build-time dependencies for completeness.
Disabled optional dependencies are not included. This file covers Cargo crates;
the separately bundled Three.js notice is in third-party-licenses.txt.

Number of distinct package versions: 60

License, copying, notice, and copyright files below are reproduced from the
published crate sources in the local Cargo registry cache, without editing their text.
Where a published crate omits a standalone license file, its manifest declaration
is recorded and the declared Apache-2.0 option is selected. The complete standard
Apache-2.0 text in those sections is reproduced from approx 0.5.1 / LICENSE.
This is a distribution notice, not a change to the upstream licenses.
